- Position 1: D (coordinates via both carboxylate oxygens)
- Position 3: D or N (coordinates via side chain)
- Position 5: D (coordinates via side chain and backbone)
- Position 7: N or D (coordinates via side chain)
- Position 9: backbone coordination (any residue, often has backbone carbonyl)
- Position 12: E (coordinates via side chain)

So the key side-chain coordinating residues are at positions 1, 3, 5, 7, and 12.
